Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Project Map\\r\\n\\r\\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\\r\\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\\r\\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\\r\\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\\r\\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\\r\\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}]

Block the push on any compile error. Set SKIP_TYPECHECK=1 to\\r\\n# bypass for an emergency push.\\r\\nif [ \\"${SKIP_TYPECHECK:-0}\\" != \\"1\\" ]; then\\r\\n  echo \\"[safe-push] Typechecking (npm run typecheck)...\\"\\r\\n  if ! npm run --silent typecheck; then\\r\\n    echo \\"[safe-push] Typecheck FAILED. Pushing.\\"\\r\\nelse\\r\\n  echo \\"[safe-push] origin/main has diverged (Lovable likely committed). Merging.\\"\\r\\n  git merge origin/main --no-edit\\r\\nfi\\r\\n\\r\\n# Push the checkout that passed the gate, including an isolated detached worktree.\\r\\ngit push origin HEAD:main\\r\\necho \\"[safe-push] Pushed $(git log -1 --format=\'%h %s\')\\"\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"054364","wall_time_seconds":2.0892616999999998,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1368,"output":"\\r\\nName\\r\\n----\\r\\n2026-10-02-celia-payment-crediting.md\\r\\nThe payment event reports zero pending webhooks. This means no outstanding deliveries, not proof of successful crediting. The parent subsequently inspected Stripe delivery `wc_1ULtZQIULVCj6rqqkFdSHw5H` at `2026-10-01 19:39:24 America/Toronto`: HTTP 200, response `{received:true,student_id:\'749e2eae-7969-464f-bf96-a4d4cfafe2bf\',grant:\'awaiting_teacher\'}`. Payment receipt and student matching succeeded. The deployed package branch deliberately stops before granting credits.\\r\\n\\r\\nDirect authenticated admin REST independently confirmed live `stripe_payments` row `bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda`, charge `ch_3ULtZLIULVCj6rqq0wLbrlFN`, status `succeeded`, match_state `matched`, offer `package-5`, grant_state `awaiting`, source `webhook`. That is the verified cause of the missing sessions.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The checkout examined first was stale\\r\\n\\r\\nThe working checkout is on `feat/calendar-strip-deeplink`, with existing unrelated edits. Its visible webhook called `apply_booking_package_payment`; this was older code than the deployed system. That initially made the case look unexplained. Git history corrected the diagnosis: commit `215936574186be9a38ed302323d54284c93b4f8a`, titled \\"Stripe payments ledger on Today: 90-day sync, grant confirmation, review queue\\", contains the exact `awaiting_teacher` response.\\r\\n\\r\\nThat commit explicitly implements `decisions/log.md`, 2026-09-27: recognized package payments wait for Joshua to click \\"Grant N passes\\" on teacher Home. The live response and live ledger match that implementation. There is no configuration flag to enable automatic package grants in this version. Joshua\'s current request asks to reconsider this deliberate prior policy.\\r\\n\\r\\n`../ai-os-projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260819075740_aaf80471-b8b9-452a-8713-02cc380f7608.sql` defines that RPC. It increments the newest pass or inserts one and records a `paid` commerce event in the same transaction. Event ID is its idempotency boundary. Its authenticated execution grant was subsequently revoked; admin JWT cannot invoke it directly.\\r\\n\\r\\nLocal gaps:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Unknown payment links return HTTP 200 with `ignored: unknown_payment_link`, without a durable diagnostic record.\\r\\n- Unmatched email creates `unmatched_paid` and returns HTTP 200 pending, but no package reconciliation worker exists locally.\\r\\n- `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` is subscribed but ignored by the handler; a delayed successful payment would not receive credits.\\r\\n- Successful package ledger records omit checkout session, amount, and currency despite schema support, which limits diagnosis and reconciliation.\\r\\n- Admins have no visible commerce-ledger policy in the inspected migrations, making the existing REST read uninformative.\\r\\n- Validation uses the payment link\'s current price/active state rather than the purchase-time facts, so later price changes or link deactivation can reject a valid purchase replay.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Reconcile the manual grant before enabling automatic grants\\r\\n\\r\\nThe manual pass\'s notes now contain the exact event, intent, charge, ledger ID, amount, payment date, and an explicit statement that this pass fulfills the purchase. This was written through `booking-pass-admin` action `update`, then independently reread. Five total, zero used, and active status remained unchanged.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe newer migration `20260927090000_stripe_payments_ledger.sql` provides `admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)`. Following parent authorization and a conditional preread of the exact intent, charge, and awaiting state, this investigation invoked it for Celia\'s ledger row. An independent reread confirmed `grant_state: dismissed`, `match_state: matched`, and the manual pass still active with five total and zero used; notes remained present. This clears the already handled purchase from the grant queue while preserving its payment record. `dismissed` is not a formal `granted` fulfillment record. **Do not call `admin_grant_stripe_payment` now**: it would add another five sessions. Its current implementation protects `granted` rows but does not reject `dismissed` rows, so future automatic grants must explicitly skip dismissed rows.\\r\\n\\r\\nMinimal policy change: preserve the existing Stripe ledger and teacher review for unmatched or unknown purchases; after successful live-mode, paid-status, known-package, price/currency, and exact account matching checks, invoke a service-role-only transactional grant function keyed by the existing Stripe payment row. It must lock the payment row, only accept `awaiting`, grant exactly once, and preserve `dismissed`/`granted` decisions. Use this shared transaction for both automatic and teacher-confirmed grants, with a separate admin-authorized reconciliation operation that can mark an existing manual pass as fulfilling a payment without changing its balance. Handle delayed payment success using the same checks. Update the prior decision explicitly when the new policy is approved, and deploy through the approved Lovable route.\\r\\n\\r\\nMeaningful verification should cover duplicate event delivery, manual fulfillment followed by delayed retry, unmatched email resolved after signup, unknown link remediation, delayed payment success, price changes after purchase, and transactional rollback when credit or receipt persistence fails. These changes involve Edge Functions/database work and require the approved Lovable deployment route before being called live.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"382ea8","wall_time_seconds":2.1010259,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2,"output":"True\\r\\n"}'}]

This supersedes the 2026-09-27 manual-confirmation requirement for confidently matched, verified paid package Checkouts. Unknown or unmatched purchases remain in teacher review. Fulfillment must be transactional and duplicate-safe, and already granted or dismissed/manual payments must not grant again. Celia's restored five sessions fulfill her 2026-10-01 payment. Authorization covers the focused backend deployment, not unrelated Lovable development or historical bulk crediting.\n'@",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:1000}));

## assistant

The fix also needs to preserve payment decisions during retries and payment syncs. I found that those paths could overwrite a dismissed payment’s state. I’m adding a transactional grant that accepts only awaiting payments, so Celia’s handled payment and previously granted payments stay protected.

store("paymentVerifyScript",`import fs from 'node:fs';
const parse = (p) => Object.fromEntries(fs.readFileSync(p,'utf8').split(/\\r?\\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(x=>x && !x.startsWith('#') && x.includes('=')).map(x=>{let i=x.indexOf('=');return [x.slice(0,i).trim(),x.slice(i+1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g,'')]}));
const e={...parse('C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/.env'),...parse('C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/.env')};
const url=e.VITE_SUPABASE_URL, key=e.VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
if(!url.includes('atrcqxpmqrynmtvvyjju')) throw new Error('Wrong backend');
const a=await fetch(url+'/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password',{method:'POST',headers:{apikey:[REDACTED],'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({email:e.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL,password:[REDACTED]})});
const auth=await a.json();if(!auth.access_token)throw new Error('Admin auth failed');
const h={apikey:[REDACTED],authorization:[REDACTED],'content-type':'application/json'};
async function read(path){let r=await fetch(url+'/rest/v1/'+path,{headers:h});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('Read failed '+r.status);return r.json();}
const payments=await read('stripe_payments?id=eq.bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda&select=id,student_id,status,match_state,grant_state,booking_pass_id,offer_slug,updated_at');
const passes=await read('booking_passes?student_id=eq.749e2eae-7969-464f-bf96-a4d4cfafe2bf&select=id,total_credits,used_credits,status,notes&order=created_at.desc');
console.log(JSON.stringify({payments,passes},null,2));`);
text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\n"+load("paymentVerifyScript")+"\n'@ | node --input-type=module",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:2500}));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 25.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"be5dec","wall_time_seconds":10.0111416,"session_id":48550,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b60c4e","wall_time_seconds":9.0723227,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":781,"output":"# New package payments add their purchased sessions\\r\\n\\r\\nApply `supabase/migrations/20261002090000_automatic_stripe_package_credits.sql` through Lovable Cloud, then deploy `booking-commerce-webhook` with the updated `_shared/stripeLedger.ts`. No other function, frontend, secret, tier, notification, or historical balance needs changing. Keep Stripe signature verification and the current webhook `verify_jwt` configuration.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe webhook validates the active payment link, mode, settled status, amount, currency, live mode, and exact student email before calling the service-only RPC. Both completed and async-payment-succeeded Checkout events use this path. Unknown links and unmatched payers remain for review. Ledger or grant failures in this validated branch return 500 for Stripe retry.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe RPC locks the payment, accepts only awaiting live matched rows with the validated Checkout/student/offer identities, and adds database offer credits atomically with the granted ledger state. It uses a per-student transaction lock so two first payments cannot create competing passes. Teacher grants share that transaction implementation. Dismissed, granted, and none rows return unchanged; the sync refresh cannot overwrite terminal grant decisions. Historical backfill never becomes auto-eligible. A brand-new pass uses a generated token hash and an existing admin as its required creator; `granted_by` stays null for automatic grants.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verify without changing real balances\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `supabase/tests/booking/stripe_package_credits.sql` through the Cloud database SQL tool as the owner after migration. It creates a synthetic user, offer, payment, and first pass inside one transaction, checks five credits exactly once, caller permissions, wrong identity, terminal states, historical source, unmatched status, and refund failure, then rolls everything back. Capture the passing notice. Never strip BEGIN/ROLLBACK or run isolated fixture statements. An existing admin role is needed as the pass creator.\\r\\n\\r\\nRun locally: `deno test supabase/functions/_shared/stripeLedger.deno.test.ts`. This checks pending-to-succeeded eligibility and concurrent terminal-state preservation. The SQL smoke suite exercises the actual database implementation; the local mocks do not substitute for it.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor live regression, first read payment `bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda` and pass `014de9d0-5d10-45da-80be-483c01aaba35`, recording grant state and total/used credits. That payment must be dismissed. Replay Stripe event `evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa` through Stripe\'s existing endpoint only after confirming the deployed awaiting-only guard. A successful response must show `grant: dismissed`; re-read and verify exactly the same pass balance. This proves the deployed webhook path while protecting Celia\'s manually credited five sessions. Do not backfill or replay any historical eligible payment.\\r\\n\\r\\nDeployment does not configure Stripe subscriptions. Verify the existing endpoint includes `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`; enable that event on the same endpoint if absent so delayed payment methods also fulfill.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"01fe8a","wall_time_seconds":4.8487626,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":177,"output":"Import-Clixml: Unexpected end of file has occurred. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 7.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"46b824","wall_time_seconds":4.2676641,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b81b39","wall_time_seconds":2.293869,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1178,"output":"-- New validated Checkout package payments credit passes automatically.\\r\\n-- No data backfill: dismissed/granted/history rows remain untouched.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_payment_id uuid, _actor uuid)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE\\r\\n  _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _creator uuid;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE id = _payment_id FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.student_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_a_student_first\'; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.status <> \'succeeded\' OR _row.amount_refunded_cents <> 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_not_settled\'; END IF;\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _row.offer_id AND provider = \'stripe\';\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'not_a_package_offer\'; END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  -- Different payments for a student also serialize, including their first pass.\\r\\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(_row.student_id::text, 0));\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _row.student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF FOUND THEN\\r\\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits, status = \'active\', expires_at = NULL WHERE id = _pass.id;\\r\\n  ELSE\\r\\n    SELECT user_id INTO _creator FROM public.user_roles WHERE role = \'admin\' ORDER BY user_id LIMIT 1;\\r\\n    _creator := COALESCE(_actor, _creator);\\r\\n    IF _creator IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'pass_creator_missing\'; END IF;\\r\\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status, token_hash, created_by)\\r\\n      VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.email, COALESCE(_row.payer_name, _row.email, \'Student\'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, \'active\',\\r\\n        encode(sha256(convert_to(gen_random_uuid()::text, \'UTF8\')), \'hex\'), _creator);\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email, amount_cents, currency)\\r\\n    VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.offer_id, \'stripe\', \'charge:\' || _row.stripe_charge_id, \'paid\', _row.email, _row.amount_cents, _row.currency)\\r\\n    ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'granted\', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = _actor, updated_at = now()\\r\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\r\\n  RETURN _row;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.grant_stripe_package_locked(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\r\\n  RETURN public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_payment_id, auth.uid());\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(_charge_id text, _checkout_session_id text, _student_id uuid, _offer_id text)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF auth.role() IS DISTINCT FROM \'service_role\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE stripe_charge_id = _charge_id FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.source <> \'webhook\' OR _row.match_state <> \'matched\'\\r\\n     OR _row.checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _checkout_session_id\\r\\n     OR _row.student_id IS DISTINCT FROM _student_id OR _row.offer_id IS DISTINCT FROM _offer_id\\r\\n     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _offer_id AND active AND provider = \'stripe\')\\r\\n  THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\r\\n  RETURN public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_row.id, NULL);\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text, text, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text, text, uuid, text) TO service_role;\\r\\n"}'}]

The ledger row above is marked \\"awaiting\\", and the teacher\\n-  // grants it from Today with one click (admin_grant_stripe_payment).\\n-  return json({ received: true, student_id: student.id, grant: \\"awaiting_teacher\\" });\\n+  // Package fulfillment is durable and retryable. The locked RPC preserves dismissed/granted/history rows.\\n+  if (!ledgerChargeId) throw new Error(\\"package_charge_missing\\");\\n+  await recordCharge(supabase, stripeSecret, ledgerChargeId, \\"webhook\\");\\n+  const { data: payment, error: grantError } = await supabase.rpc(\\"auto_grant_stripe_payment\\", {\\n+    _charge_id: ledgerChargeId,\\n+    _checkout_session_id: session.id,\\n+    _student_id: student.id,\\n+    _offer_id: offer.id,\\n+  });\\n+  if (grantError) throw grantError;\\n+  return json({ received: true, student_id: student.id, grant: payment?.grant_state ?? \\"review\\" });\\n }\\n \\n Deno.serve(async (req) => {\\n@@ -467,7 +476,7 @@ async function chargeIdForLedger(event: any, secret: string): Promise<string | n\\n   if (event.type.startsWith(\\"charge.dispute.\\")) return typeof obj.charge === \\"string\\" ? obj.charge : null;\\n   if (event.type.startsWith(\\"charge.\\")) return obj.id ?? null;\\n   if (event.type.startsWith(\\"refund.\\")) return typeof obj.charge === \\"string\\" ? obj.charge : null;\\n-  if (event.type === \\"checkout.session.completed\\" && obj.payment_status === \\"paid\\" && typeof obj.payment_intent === \\"string\\") {\\n+  if ((event.type === \\"checkout.session.completed\\" || event.type === \\"checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded\\") && obj.payment_status === \\"paid\\" && typeof obj.payment_intent === \\"string\\") {\\n     const intent = await stripeGet(secret, `/payment_intents/${obj.payment_intent}`);\\n     return typeof intent.latest_charge === \\"string\\" ? intent.latest_charge : null;\\n   }\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c61343","wall_time_seconds":6.2944057,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1213,"output":"-- Run as database owner after migration. Entire fixture transaction rolls back.\\r\\n-- Synthetic user/offer/charges only; no real student or payment is updated.\\r\\nBEGIN;\\r\\nSELECT set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'service_role\', true);\\r\\nSELECT set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\"role\\":\\"service_role\\"}\', true);\\r\\nDO $$\\r\\nDECLARE\\r\\n  student uuid := gen_random_uuid();\\r\\n  offer text := \'credit-smoke-\' || gen_random_uuid()::text;\\r\\n  charge text := \'ch_smoke_\' || gen_random_uuid()::text;\\r\\n  payment_id uuid;\\r\\n  result public.stripe_payments;\\r\\n  balance integer;\\r\\n  state text;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF has_function_privilege(\'authenticated\', \'public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text,text,uuid,text)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\r\\n     OR has_function_privilege(\'anon\', \'public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text,text,uuid,text)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\r\\n     OR has_function_privilege(\'service_role\', \'public.grant_stripe_package_locked(uuid,uuid)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\r\\n  THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unsafe_function_permissions\'; END IF;\\r\\n  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, email) VALUES (student, student::text || \'@example.invalid\');\\r\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_offers (id, label, provider, provider_ref, url, credits)\\r\\n    VALUES (offer, \'Rollback smoke test\', \'stripe\', \'plink_\' || offer, \'https://example.invalid\', 5);\\r\\n  INSERT INTO public.stripe_payments (stripe_charge_id, checkout_session_id, amount_cents, currency, status, paid_at, livemode, student_id, match_state, offer_id, grant_state, source, auto_grant_eligible)\\r\\n    VALUES (charge, \'cs_smoke\', 20000, \'usd\', \'succeeded\', now(), true, student, \'matched\', offer, \'awaiting\', \'webhook\', true) RETURNING id INTO payment_id;\\r\\n\\r\\n  -- Caller/identity failures do not mutate balances.\\r\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'authenticated\', true);\\r\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\"role\\":\\"authenticated\\"}\', true);\\r\\n  BEGIN\\r\\n    PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'non_service_caller_accepted\';\\r\\n  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\\r\\n    IF SQLERRM <> \'forbidden\' THEN RAISE; END IF;\\r\\n  END;\\r\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'service_role\', true);\\r\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\"role\\":\\"service_role\\"}\', true);\\r\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'wrong_session\', student, offer);\\r\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'mismatch_credited\'; END IF;\\r\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'granted\' OR result.granted_credits <> 5 OR result.granted_by IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'grant_failed\'; END IF;\\r\\n  PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n  SELECT total_credits INTO balance FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student;\\r\\n  IF balance <> 5 OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student) <> 1 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'duplicate_or_first_pass_failed\'; END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  FOR state IN SELECT unnest(ARRAY[\'dismissed\',\'none\']) LOOP\\r\\n    UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = state WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n    result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n    IF result.grant_state <> state THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'protected_state_changed:%\', state; END IF;\\r\\n  END LOOP;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'awaiting\', auto_grant_eligible = false WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'pre_rollout_payment_credited\'; END IF;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET auto_grant_eligible = true WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'awaiting\', match_state = \'unmatched\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unmatched_credited\'; END IF;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET match_state = \'matched\', source = \'backfill\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'history_credited\'; END IF;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET source = \'webhook\', status = \'refunded\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\r\\n  BEGIN\\r\\n    PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\r\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'refund_credited\';\\r\\n  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\\r\\n    IF SQLERRM <> \'payment_not_settled\' THEN RAISE; END IF;\\r\\n  END;\\r\\n  SELECT total_credits INTO balance FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student;\\r\\n  IF balance <> 5 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'protected_payment_added_credits\'; END IF;\\r\\n  RAISE NOTICE \'stripe_package_credits: all checks passed; rollback follows\';\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\nROLLBACK;\\r\\n"}'}]

END IF;\\r\\n  IF NOT _row.auto_grant_eligible OR _row.source <> \'webhook\' OR _row.match_state <> \'matched\'\\r\\n     OR _row.checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _checkout_session_id\\r\\n     OR _row.student_id IS DISTINCT FROM _student_id OR _row.offer_id IS DISTINCT FROM _offer_id\\r\\n     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _offer_id AND active AND provider = \'stripe\')\\r\\n  THEN RETURN _row; echo ok\\\\\\"}\\">\\n</lov-tool-use>\\n\\n<lov-tool-use id=\\"toolu_vrtx_01Y2WjHVsAea5qE9hfAFMC2q\\" name=\\"lov_database--migration\\" data=\\"{\\\\\\"name\\\\\\": \\\\\\"automatic_stripe_package_credits\\\\\\", \\\\\\"query\\\\\\": \\\\\\"-- New validated Checkout package payments credit passes automatically.\\\\\\\\n-- No data backfill: dismissed/granted/history rows remain untouched.\\\\\\\\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_payments ADD COLUMN auto_grant_eligible boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;\\\\\\\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_payment_id uuid, _actor uuid)\\\\\\\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\\\\\\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\\\\\\\nDECLARE\\\\\\\\n  _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\\\\\\\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\\\\\\\\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\\\\\\\\n  _creator uuid;\\\\\\\\nBEGIN\\\\\\\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE id = _payment_id FOR UPDATE;\\\\\\\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  IF _row.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  IF _row.student_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_a_student_first\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  IF _row.status <> \'succeeded\' OR _row.amount_refunded_cents <> 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_not_settled\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _row.offer_id AND provider = \'stripe\';\\\\\\\\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'not_a_package_offer\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n  -- Different payments for a student also serialize, including their first pass.\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(_row.student_id::text, 0));\\\\\\\\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _row.student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\\\\\\\\n  IF FOUND THEN\\\\\\\\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits, status = \'active\', expires_at = NULL WHERE id = _pass.id;\\\\\\\\n  ELSE\\\\\\\\n    SELECT user_id INTO _creator FROM public.user_roles WHERE role = \'admin\' ORDER BY user_id LIMIT 1;\\\\\\\\n    _creator := COALESCE(_actor, _creator);\\\\\\\\n    IF _creator IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'pass_creator_missing\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status, token_hash, created_by)\\\\\\\\n      VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.email, COALESCE(_row.payer_name, _row.email, \'Student\'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, \'active\',\\\\\\\\n        encode(sha256(convert_to(gen_random_uuid()::text, \'UTF8\')), \'hex\'), _creator);\\\\\\\\n  END IF;\\\\\\\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email, amount_cents, currency)\\\\\\\\n    VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.offer_id, \'stripe\', \'charge:\' || _row.stripe_charge_id, \'paid\', _row.email, _row.amount_cents, _row.currency)\\\\\\\\n    ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'granted\', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = _actor, updated_at = now()\\\\\\\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\\\\\\\n  RETURN _row;\\\\\\\\nEND;\\\\\\\\n$$;\\\\\\\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.grant_stripe_package_locked(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\\\\\\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\\\\\\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\\\\\\\nBEGIN\\\\\\\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  RETURN public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_payment_id, auth.uid());\\\\\\\\nEND;\\\\\\\\n$$;\\\\\\\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, service_role;\\\\\\\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(_charge_id text, _checkout_session_id text, _student_id uuid, _offer_id text)\\\\\\\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\\\\\\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\\\\\\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\\\\\\\nBEGIN\\\\\\\\n  IF auth.role() IS DISTINCT FROM \'service_role\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE stripe_charge_id = _charge_id FOR UPDATE;\\\\\\\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  IF _row.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  IF NOT _row.auto_grant_eligible OR _row.source <> \'webhook\' OR _row.match_state <> \'matched\'\\\\\\\\n     OR _row.checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _checkout_session_id\\\\\\\\n     OR _row.student_id IS DISTINCT FROM _student_id OR _row.offer_id IS DISTINCT FROM _offer_id\\\\\\\\n     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _offer_id AND active AND provider = \'stripe\')\\\\\\\\n  THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  RETURN public.grant_stripe_package_locked(_row.id, NULL);\\\\\\\\nEND;\\\\\\\\n$$;\\\\\\\\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text, text, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\\\\\\\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text, text, uuid, text) TO service_role;\\\\\\"}\\">\\n</lov-tool-use>\\n\\n<lov-tool-use id=\\"toolu_vrtx_01JQkMq7jbTdMzHSgrXxnoam\\" name=\\"supabase--deploy_edge_functions\\" data=\\"{\\\\\\"function_names\\\\\\": [\\\\\\"booking-commerce-webhook\\\\\\"]}\\">\\n</lov-tool-use>\\nNow the rollback smoke test.\\n<lov-tool-use id=\\"toolu_vrtx_01XWy4gVdiMF9PLsoBkAh1Wu\\" name=\\"supabase--run_sql\\" data=\\"{\\\\\\"description\\\\\\": \\\\\\"- Runs the self-cleaning package-credit test using a made-up student, offer and payment\\\\\\\\n- Everything it creates is undone at the end; no real student or payment is changed\\\\\\", \\\\\\"query\\\\\\": \\\\\\"BEGIN;\\\\\\\\nSELECT set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'service_role\', true);\\\\\\\\nSELECT set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"service_role\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}\', true);\\\\\\\\nDO $$\\\\\\\\nDECLARE\\\\\\\\n  student uuid := gen_random_uuid();\\\\\\\\n  offer text := \'credit-smoke-\' || gen_random_uuid()::text;\\\\\\\\n  charge text := \'ch_smoke_\' || gen_random_uuid()::text;\\\\\\\\n  payment_id uuid;\\\\\\\\n  result public.stripe_payments;\\\\\\\\n  balance integer;\\\\\\\\n  state text;\\\\\\\\nBEGIN\\\\\\\\n  IF has_function_privilege(\'authenticated\', \'public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text,text,uuid,text)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\\\\\\\n     OR has_function_privilege(\'anon\', \'public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(text,text,uuid,text)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\\\\\\\n     OR has_function_privilege(\'service_role\', \'public.grant_stripe_package_locked(uuid,uuid)\', \'EXECUTE\')\\\\\\\\n  THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unsafe_function_permissions\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, email) VALUES (student, student::text || \'@example.invalid\');\\\\\\\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_offers (id, label, provider, provider_ref, url, credits)\\\\\\\\n    VALUES (offer, \'Rollback smoke test\', \'stripe\', \'plink_\' || offer, \'https://example.invalid\', 5);\\\\\\\\n  INSERT INTO public.stripe_payments (stripe_charge_id, checkout_session_id, amount_cents, currency, status, paid_at, livemode, student_id, match_state, offer_id, grant_state, source, auto_grant_eligible)\\\\\\\\n    VALUES (charge, \'cs_smoke\', 20000, \'usd\', \'succeeded\', now(), true, student, \'matched\', offer, \'awaiting\', \'webhook\', true) RETURNING id INTO payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'authenticated\', true);\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"authenticated\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}\', true);\\\\\\\\n  BEGIN\\\\\\\\n    PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'non_service_caller_accepted\';\\\\\\\\n  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\\\\\\\\n    IF SQLERRM <> \'forbidden\' THEN RAISE; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  END;\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claim.role\', \'service_role\', true);\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM set_config(\'request.jwt.claims\', \'{\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"service_role\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}\', true);\\\\\\\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'wrong_session\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'mismatch_credited\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'granted\' OR result.granted_credits <> 5 OR result.granted_by IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'grant_failed\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  SELECT total_credits INTO balance FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student;\\\\\\\\n  IF balance <> 5 OR (SELECT count(*) FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student) <> 1 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'duplicate_or_first_pass_failed\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  FOR state IN SELECT unnest(ARRAY[\'dismissed\',\'none\']) LOOP\\\\\\\\n    UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = state WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n    result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n    IF result.grant_state <> state THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'protected_state_changed:%\', state; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  END LOOP;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'awaiting\', auto_grant_eligible = false WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'pre_rollout_payment_credited\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET auto_grant_eligible = true WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET grant_state = \'awaiting\', match_state = \'unmatched\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unmatched_credited\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET match_state = \'matched\', source = \'backfill\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  result := public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n  IF result.grant_state <> \'awaiting\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'history_credited\'; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments SET source = \'webhook\', status = \'refunded\' WHERE id = payment_id;\\\\\\\\n  BEGIN\\\\\\\\n    PERFORM public.auto_grant_stripe_payment(charge, \'cs_smoke\', student, offer);\\\\\\\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'refund_credited\';\\\\\\\\n  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\\\\\\\\n    IF SQLERRM <> \'payment_not_settled\' THEN RAISE; END IF;\\\\\\\\n  END;\\\\\\\\n  SELECT total_credits INTO balance FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = student;\\\\\\\\n  IF balance <> 5 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'protected_payment_added_credits\'; ## assistant

The backend is deployed. Lovable used **3.1 credits** for the single request. The database test passed and rolled back its fixtures; Celia still has five sessions. I’m doing one final live replay of her already-handled payment to verify the deployed webhook cannot credit it twice.


## Tool: exec

const baseline=load("paymentVerifyScript").replace("status,match_state,grant_state","status,match_state,grant_state,auto_grant_eligible").replace("console.log(JSON.stringify({payments,passes},null,2));","");
const script=baseline+`
if(payments.length!==1 || payments[0].grant_state!=='dismissed' || payments[0].auto_grant_eligible!==false)throw new Error('Manual payment guard not verified; no replay sent');
const target=passes.find(x=>x.id==='014de9d0-5d10-45da-80be-483c01aaba35');
if(!target || target.total_credits!==5 || target.status!=='active')throw new Error('Manual pass not verified; no replay sent');
const er=await fetch('https://api.stripe.com/v1/events/evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa',{headers:{authorization:[REDACTED]}});
if(!er.ok)throw new Error('Stripe event read failed');
const event=await er.json();
if(event.id!=='evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa'||event.type!=='checkout.session.completed'||!event.livemode||event.data.object.customer_details.email!=='celiacutler@gmail.com'||event.data.object.amount_total!==20000||event.data.object.payment_status!=='paid')throw new Error('Original payment facts differ; no replay sent');
if(!e.STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET)throw new Error('Webhook signing credential unavailable; no replay sent');
const {createHmac}=await import('node:crypto');
const raw=JSON.stringify(event),timestamp=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000);
const signature=createHmac('sha256',e.STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(timestamp+'.'+raw).digest('hex');
const response=await fetch(url+'/functions/v1/booking-commerce-webhook?provider=stripe',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json','stripe-signature':'t='+timestamp+',v1='+signature},body:raw,signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000)});
const result=await response.json();
const afterPayments=await read('stripe_payments?id=eq.bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda&select=id,student_id,status,match_state,grant_state,auto_grant_eligible');
const afterPasses=await read('booking_passes?student_id=eq.749e2eae-7969-464f-bf96-a4d4cfafe2bf&select=id,total_credits,used_credits,status,notes&order=created_at.desc');
const unchanged=JSON.stringify(passes)===JSON.stringify(afterPasses);
console.log(JSON.stringify({http_status:response.status,response:result,payment:afterPayments[0],pass_balance_unchanged:unchanged,passes:afterPasses.map(({id,total_credits,used_credits,status})=>({id,total_credits,used_credits,status}))},null,2));
if(!response.ok || result.grant!=='dismissed' || !unchanged || afterPayments[0].grant_state!=='dismissed')throw new Error('Live duplicate-credit regression failed; do not retry blindly');
`;
store("liveReplayScript",script);
text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\n"+script+"\n'@ | node --input-type=module",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:3000,login:false}));
